###### YEAR RPT Accomplishment Rate

With the above stated rates including the low debt service ratio and savings for the last 5 years, it clearly manifests the Municipality’s capacity to carry out and realize basic services, infrastructure projects and other programs/projects for a given year without resorting to Bank Loans, bond floatation and business ventures. Thru local revenue collection efforts, the LGU gained an exceptional performance obtaining a rate of 110.33%, which is higher than the average 1st class municipality & national average rate.

#### CLIMATE AND DISASTER RISK ASSESSMENT (INSTITUTIONAL FACILITIES)

###### A. INSTITUTIONAL FACILITIES AFFECTED BY LANDSLIDE

All barangay halls are exposed to landslide with very low vulnerability, low susceptibility, and risk level. The Bureau of Fire Protection, Philippine National Police, Post Office, and Bureau of Jail Management and Penology (BJMP)-Carmona located inside the DILG-Compound in Barangay Maduya are also susceptible to low landslide affecting 1,065.5 sq. m. Other Government-owned buildings susceptible to landslide are the Municipal Disaster Risk Reduction and Management Office (MDRRMO), Carmona Municipal Hall, BML Building I and II, with a total land area of 1,794,4 sq. m. affected. The Carmona Public Market (CPM) and Municipal Trial Court are also exposed to landslides with low susceptibility and low-risk levels.

Due to occasional or slight chance of landslide occurrence, the following critical facilities have low-risk levels. This hazard will affect 2,037 individuals, of which 1,334 are barangay personnel, while 703 are municipal personnel (468 males and 235 females).

![image 1647](<673810152-CLUP-Carmona-Volume-3-Final_images/imageFile1647.png>)

|Out-dated official municipality website|The production and dissemination of digital information were focused on Facebook which resulted in the neglect of the municipal website.| |Limited/outdated digital information accessible to scholars, members of the media, tourists, investors and the general public, which may leave an impression that the LGU does not comply with policies related to freedom of information| Form a core team and conduct skills training that will update and manage the official municipal website|
|Lack of innovation in terms of information dissemination|The production and dissemination of information are still limited to Facebook posts, audio-visual presentations, tarpaulins and other printed media.| |Limited audience reach and lack of interest in repetitive format and content of Facebook posts, tarpaulins and other printed media| Launch a weekly news/magazine program that will air via Facebook Live, featuring not only the LGU but the entire Carmona community. The episodes can be shared on other social media platforms and the official municipal website.<br> Produce a quarterly community magazine in lieu of an annual brochure<br> Utilize the community cable and launch a weekly television program<br>|

### Chapter 8 : INTEGRATED SECTOR ANALYSIS MATRIX

A. INTEGRATED SOCIAL ANALYSIS MATRIX

|Priority Issues/ Problems|Possible Intervention (Policies, Programs/Projects)<br><br>|Responsible Agency|
|---|---|---|
|EDUCATION (ELEMENTARY)| | |
|Presence of at least 5% of the enrolment are considered struggling readers| Training of teachers in handling pupils with learning difficulties in literacy<br> Provision of appropriate textbook or reading materials<br> Coordination with parents for proper handling of pupils with learning difficulties in literacy<br>|Carmona-DepEd District Office|
|Low number of enrollees who participated in the online mode of learning| Seek assistance from the LGU on the provision of public-use internet<br> Partnership and cooperation to stakeholders as to the delivery and retrieval of students’ output<br>|LGU/ Carmona-DepEd District Office|
|Incomplete reference books| Seek assistance from the Regional Office and Central Office for the production and distribution of adequate number of reference books|Carmona-DepEd District Office|
|Congestion in the number of pupils inside the Carmona National High School| Establishment of Carmona Science High School with appropriate laboratories|LGU/DepEd|
|JUNIOR HIGH SCHOOL| | |
|Presence of low reading ability (2% of learners population were non-readers)| Provide appropriate textbook or reading materials<br> Provision of speech laboratories<br>|Carmona-DepEd District Office|
|ALTERNATIVE LEARNING SYSTEM (ALS)| | |
|Increased rate of Out-of-School Youth and Adults| Advocacy and Social Mobilization of ALS program in every barangay<br> Barangay, LGU, Education, parents involvement to address the issues and concerns<br>|Carmona-DepEd District Office|

![image 1730](<673810152-CLUP-Carmona-Volume-3-Final_images/imageFile1730.png>)

|---|---|---|
|YOUTH DEVELOPMENT| | |
|Presence of psychological issues among the youth including depression, suicidal tendencies, psychosocial problems, etc.| Healthy Youth: Mental and Emotional Health (Equip the youth with knowledge on mental health)| |
|Insufficient knowledge on sexual responsibility and reproductive health| Healthy Youth: Kabataan Maging Responsible! (Give access to youth on knowledge regarding sexual responsibility and reproductive health| |
|Presence of gender-based inequalities among the youth| Empowered Youth: Kabilang Ka! (Conduct seminar or film showing to understand the different SOGIE)| |
|Presence of drug dependent youth, youth offenders, and delinquent youth| Carmona Youth: Supporting Peace and Strong Institution| |
|Presence of case on Violence Against Women and Children| Empowered Youth: Kabilang Ka! (Conduct seminar on VAW and Children)| |
|HEALTH| | |
|Presence of households without toilet and with unsanitary toilet facilities| Provision of sanitary toilet<br> Awareness on Zero Open Defacation<br>|Municipal Health Office|
|Declining number of available burial slots in public cemetery| Provide alternative sanitary disposal of human remains|Municipal Health Office|
|Prevalence of Non-Communicable Diseases (Focus on smoking, drug and alcohol)| Strengthen all aspects of healthy lifestyle promotion<br> Bolster awareness campaign on NCDs using modern technology<br> Introduce measures on alcohol and tobacco control through demand reduction and supply restriction<br>|Municipal Health Office|

|Increasing incidence of dog and cat bites| Heighten Rabies Control Program<br> Establishment of Animal Bite Center<br>|Municipal Health Office|
|Absence of Out Patient Department (OPD)-Based Drug Rehabilitation Center (Non Residential)| Completion of basic requirements for OPD-Based Rehabilitation Center (Non-Residential)|Municipal Health Office|
|Presence of vaccine preventable childhood diseases| Defaulter Tracing for missed children through updating of Community Health Team Columnar|Municipal Health Office|
|Limited acceptance of referrals to other advanced medical facilities| Utilize the top floor of the PBC for trauma and incubated patients<br> Construction of operating room<br> Outsourcing of specialized doctors<br> MOA with another referral hospital<br>|Pagamutang Bayan ng Carmona (PBC()|
|Prevalence of undetected PTB cases| Intensify awareness of community on TB<br> Capacitate BHWs and FHWs on case notification<br>|Municipal Health Office|
|Increasing HIV cases| Strengthening STI/HIV/AIDS Control Program thru Awareness campaign to Most At Risk Population (MARPs), HIV Counseling & Testing, disease barrier distribution and Capability building<br> Activation of STI/HIV/AIDS Council<br>|Municipal Health Office|

|COVID-19 outbreak| COVID-19 Response and Mitigation Program<br> Awareness campaign/public health messaging<br> Minimum public health standards<br> Physical/mental health resiliency promotion, monitoring and treatment<br> Vaccination<br>|Municipal Health Office|
